A quick synopsis: I was hit by a car walking through a parking lot, the rear window was broken, so police were called, when they arrived, they arrested me, and they denied me medical attention. I was charged, but officers didn't take my statement, so I wrote my own statement and had it notarized. I gave that statement and my medical records to the district attorneys office and I agreed to go to trial without a lawyer. I had trail and was found guilty, I was order to pay for the damaged window. judge Hadley said i was owed a copy of the receipt, so I was ordered to return weeks later and the ada provided a document and stated that this driver paid for this window out of her own pocket, but I was not allowed to have a copy. 
Then I would learn that this driver did not have car insurance, I would try to report it to multiple lawyers, Springfield's citizen police complaint department, the probation department, the district attorney's office, the mayor's office, the Springfield Police Department, the ACLU, the Department of Justice, the attorney general's office, the Massachusetts state police, the department of industrial accidents, insurance companies, the FBI and even our local news stations. Not one group could offer me any help or an explanation as to why these types of things were being allowed to continue.

Next I would learn that this window was never paid for, that the receipt ada Johnston handed in was a forgery and learned my statement had been altered and someone electronically forged my signature, so I reported this to the district attorneys office, Jon Gay in the clerks office, Springfield's citizen police complaint department, The mayor's office, the Massachusetts State Police the attorney general's office, the governor's office the ACLU again. 
Then I receive court papers stating that I am to appear in court over a forgery charge, I mistakenly thought that this was about discovering that my statement had been altered and electronically forged and that window receipt was fake. My first court appointed attorney Nolan thought my case was amazing, until he noticed who was involved. then I was provided attorney Elaine Pourinski, Soon after this my mental health declined, attorney Pourinski was well aware of everything going on at the time and took advantage of this fact that I tried to commit suicide multiple times. It's pretty obvious now that she was behind that older gentleman placing a gun to my forehead and taking files from me in the hallway to her office right before our last meeting before I was supposed to have trial. She coerced me into pleading guilty, she Knew that I was an emotional train wreck and then I was worried about the safety and well being of my family and friends. She even used her own safety and the fact that she was about to have knee surgery as an excuse to not get police involved, so that none of these violent individuals searched her out and hurt her or her family. 
I did plead guilty in February of 2016 but I did not say what every one had demanded of me, So I haven't been able to acquire a copy of the audio from that hearing.


In order for the story to make sense I need to set the stage.



I usually start this story after I got hit by the car but I'm going to start this well he was October or November of 2012 I'm going to have to fix this and find a picture I had surgery on my left wrist I had torn ligaments in my surgeon said it looked like Swiss cheese and I couldn't afford to take time off of work so I went back to work and at the time my boss was kind of a crabby **** and he thought he'd be funny and give me a really hard job to do with one arm and I ended up hurting myself because I didn't know I had a genetic skeletal muscle disorder and my hyperthyroidism makes me weak and doctors said that it might affect my joints or I could have an undiagnosed joint disorder. So I was on workers comp at the time that I got hit and I was super worried when police denied me medical attention that that was going to affect my worker's cup, because I've never had anything like that happened to me before. 

find surgery and injury dates???







Someone in the D.A.'s office altered the notarized statement I gave them, they deleted 2 pages
and electronically forged my signature and had that entered into evidence. Someone altered the receipt the D.A.'s office presented as proof that Kishana Johnson paid for
her window to be replaced herself. Actual bill was $245 and Kishana never returned to pay for it. I was able to get a statement from the manager at the window company and a copy of the paperwork. The signatures on the fake receipt and the signatures on the check Kishana cashed
and a restraining order she was able to get again me don't match. 5/10/13 D.A.'s office present receipt for window and stated Kishana Johnson paid it out of pocket. I wasn't able to see this paper. I asked judge hadley for Kishana Johnson's insurance information and judge hadley stated he couldn't help me or get involved. I wasn't provided with a copy of the receipt, the D.A.'s office said it was because it had insurance information on it and
judge hadley denied my request in court. 2/6/14 1 got a restraining order against Kishana Johnson. She was driving past my house daily
and I felt threatened 2/12/14 I had another court date for that restraining order. I was attacked on Philips st, on my way to court and needed medical attention. This was less than 100ft from my home 1-1-14 mant tomes reparter
fistend notpayer fer her undow, didnt see pad is 6/13/14 discovered Kishana never paid for her window, and the price was actually $245.94, not
the $311.51 the D.A.'s office told judge hadley.
7/2/14 charged with forgery
taxed from Staghs 5.21-14
went to cort 5-22-14 with ovurt order, refused color copies 9/5/14 discovered Kishana Johnson lived on Phillips st about 100ft from where I was attacked. I
tried reporting this to the police and courts. But no one cared 9/9/15 Kishana Johnson was able to get a restraining order against me because I reported her
for driving without insurance and with a rejection sticker for safety. 10/1/14 I noticed the signature on the restraining order didn't match the receipt I obtained from the window repair shop, so I went to court and got a copy on the check Kishana Johnson cashed after I paid $311 for restitution, the signature on the restraining order and check didn't match
the signature on the receipts for the window.



2/5/16 | was coerced into pleading guilty for the forgery charge. My lawyer Elaine Pourinski told me I had no chance at winning my case. That Kishana has a cousin in the clerks office and a sister in the D.A.'s office, she constantly brought up how someone already tried to kill me once and that I'm as good as dead if I keep fighting to prove my innocence. She told me I have no choice but to plead guilty or my family could be next. I was mentally unfit at the time due to hyperthyroidism and had been admitted to the psych ward on 3 occasions due to self injury, the last was 2 weeks before being coerced into pleading guilty. I was in no shape to make any decisions and the things Elaine pourinski said to me left me in fear of my own life and the safety and well-being of my family. She told me to stop looking into my case and I haven't don't
anything about it since. I believe the same person who altered and electronically forged my signature and forged Kishana's signature on the window repair bills and then presented the d.a. with a fake paid bill and raised the price is the same person who forged that court order I was coerced to plead
guilty to. I was told that the Charges should've been a Misdemenor
it is under #250.

On 1-16-2013 at about 2:45 P.M., Officer Thomas Douglas and I arrested a Richard Stebbins of 54 Hope St Springfield, Massachusetts., D.O.B. of 10-08-1978 for the charges of 1) Malicious damage to a motor vehicle
and 2) Disorderly conduct. Subject was arrested in the parking lot of The Walmart Store at 1105 Boston Rd.
Springfield, Massachusetts. At about 2:07 P.M. on 1-16-2013,Officers Thomas Douglas and I were dispatched to The Walmart Store at 1105 Boston Rd.for a subject who had smashed a car mirror and car window. Upon our arrival we were met
by the victim K
who was in the parking lot of the Walmart standing by her 2003 Acura MDX,
color white, Massachusetts registration # 249
J
stated that she was operating her vehicle in the parking lot when a pedestrian (Stebbins) maliciously and intentionally hit her passenger side mirror then smashed a gallon of milk he was carrying on the passenger side of her car. This caused milk to splash on all over the side of her vehicle. When Johnson stopped her vehicle, Stebbins punched out her rear widow with his fists causing it to shatter. Johnson stated that
the subject responsible was standing in the parking lot and pointed him(Stebbins) out to officers.
Officers walked over to Stebbins and placed him under arrest. Visible were cuts and blood on his right
hand from having punched out the window. The incident was also witnessed by a D
who was
walking in the parking lot at the time.
Detective Reuben Borrero responded and took photos of the scene. A knife found on Stebbins was tagged into the property room for safe keeping. A video disc recording of the incident was turned over by Walmart
Security and tagged into evidence# 342656
Lt. Moynihan notified.
Respectfully submitted,
Officer Thomas C. Sheehan #065822



















Notarized Statement I wrote in Emergency Room before court.
Even though you can see it is notarized. I will learn it has been altered in the 2014.
I was hurting, i was forced to leave the Hospital against doctors orders because I wanted /needed to get my statement notarized before I went to court that morning.

1-17-2013




On Wednesday January 16th at 14:03:58 I began my exit from Walmart in Springfield, MA

the weather conditions were an icy rain with a slight wind.. I walked toward the row of

 cars where I was parked and started on from what my perspective was the right side of the

 row. In the meantime a vehicle began backing out of a space as I was walking towards it.

 I don't believe they saw me, but they were backing out slow enough that I felt safe in

 crossing over to the left side of the row, my car was parked on the left side, farther away

 from the front of the store, so crossing seemed the logical thing to do. I made it to the left

 side of the row before the vehicle backing up finished reversing, At which point I looked

 up to see what was in front of me. I saw a group of approximately six people on the same

 side I was on, a woman and some younger children and I saw a white SUV slowed almost

 to a stop in the middle 1/3 of the isle approximately 30 feet away from me with the driver

 on what appeared to be a cell phone. So I continued my walk towards my car and the

 group of children looking down because of the icy conditions and seeing

that there was presently trash thrown on the ground by customers of Walmart and more


importantly I didn't want to slip and fall on ice covered pavement. A few moments later I

 was struck by the right mirror and the body of the same SUV that had been a safe distance

 away shortly before, I never saw it coming, the driver must have swerved towards me

 because I didn't weave into the middle of the road while walking. the mirror hit my right

 bicep and the body of the SUV hit the gallon of milk I had in my hand causing it to hit my

 right leg and twist it to the point that the milk jugs handle split and cut my right hand, I

 felt a huge surge of pain and tearing in my right shoulder joint and right bicep and I felt

 tearing in my right wrist, the pain was instant and I began seeing stars, the impact though

 it wasn't in my opinion very fast still caused me to lose my balance and the white SUV's

 rear right tire ran over the side of my right foot and it was




at this moment that the driver chose to stop trapping my right shoe under the edge of the

tire (I didn't know it at the time, but her tire only ran over my shoe and not my flesh and

 bone foot). So in a panic yelled move your car you're on my foot and I swung my right

 elbow at the rear passenger side of the SUV hoping to get the drivers attention, the milk

 jug I was holdings remaining contents went everywhere and the jug went flying and I was

 yanking as hard as I could to get my leg free and then I yelled move your car again and

 swung my elbow at the side of the vehicle again and while I was swinging I was able to

 yank my foot free, but I stumbled and my elbow hit the bottom right corner of the rear

 windshield instead causing it to shatter (which shouldn't have happened, it was either the

 luckiest shot in the world or there was already a defect in the window), at that point I

 could see through the now broken window and there was a cell phone in the drivers right

 hand and I yelled- get off your cell phone, you just hit me with your mirror and stopped

 your car on my foot, watch where you're driving. the driver said something either to me or

 to them person on the phone, but I don't recall what they said because I was so freaked

 out. the  driver then began to drive away, so I turned to walk to my car (I was in so much

 pain that I wasn't in any condition to chase the vehicle down and a moment later I heard

 yelling  behind me, I then turned around to see that the driver had decided to stop and I

 then yelled  call the police and we'll deal with this when they get here and I started

 walking towards my car because I didn't have my cell phone on me to call police and I

 knew there was no  point in arguing with this person about it and seeing that her window

 was broken in my  attempt to free my foot from under her tire, I didn't want to approach

 her and make her  feel that I was in anyway placing her in danger or that I was trying to

 intimidate her. The driver  yelled some more and I yelled back call the police and then

 we'll talk. I then placed my  remaining groceries into my car and grabbed a fishing vest I

 had in my trunk to help stop the bleeding on my right hand. The pain




in my wrist and shoulder was building every moment and I could feel swelling in my right

 bicep where the mirror hit me as I stood around waiting for officers to show up. while

 waiting I was approached by a few customers and Walmart security all asking if I was

 okay. I explained to them that I had been hit by the white SUV that was now parked in the

 isle towards the store and that I was in severe pain and I felt dizzy. I explained to the

 security guard that I had a previous injury to my right wrist and had surgery on my right

 shoulder, but up until the moment I was hit, I had been totally pain free in these areas

 because of therapy with my wrist and a successful surgery to correct the issue with my

 right shoulder. I asked the security guard to tell the police this too. I didn't want anyone

 thinking I was trying to fake an injury or that I was trying to claim to have been in 100%

 perfect condition before this incident happened. After what felt like forever the police

 arrived. I stayed a good distance away, so that the driver of the SUV could give their side

 of the story without me interrupting and I thought I was going to be able to give my side

 of  the story. so the police officers talked to the driver and a white officer then approached

 me . he never identified himself as an officer or said his name, the first thing he said  was,

white officer- what the fuck is wrong with you. me- that driver just hit me with their side

 mirror injuring my right shoulder, bicep and wrist and broke a gallon of milk in my hand

 causing it to cut me and then stopped her vehicle on my right foot and that in my attempt

 to get the drivers attention to move the vehicle off my foot, I accidentally broke their rear

 windshield. ( though I honestly don't think he paid attention to a word I said.) white

 officer- do you have a job me- yes I have a job, but I am out of work because I hurt my

 right trapezius sliding boxes on a job white officer- are you using drugs me- no, I haven't

 taken any pain medication because of that injury.




At which point the white officer began huffing and puffing ( mumbling under his breath).


He waited a few more moments and again asked if i had a job or was on drugs in a rude

 disrespectful manor. I told him again that yes I have a job and no i wasn't on drugs. it was

 at this point I told him I was in a lot of pain and I had previously treated injuries to my

 right shoulder and wrist that have been severely aggravated by the impact of the SUV and

 that I also told this to Walmart security, the white officer then informed me I was under

 arrest, I asked what the charges were and he ignored the question. his partner black

 officer) approached from my right white officer then asked for my hands. I told him that I

 have torn ligaments in both of my wrists and showed him the surgery scars on my left

 hand and told him I need to see a doctor, that I was in pain. both officers ignored my

 statements about my injuries even though I have fresh visible surgical scars to prove this. I

 was cuffed and I told both officers that the cuffs were to tight and I could feel the

 ligaments in both wrists tearing from the pressure.. they both ignored me and placed me in

 the police cruiser. the black officer sat in the drivers seat while the white officer

was outside the car finishing whatever he was doing. I repeated to the black officer that I

 need medical attention. the black officer told me I'll see a doctor soon. the white officer

 then entered the passenger door with papers in his hands. the white officer then stated they

 didn't want to arrest me cause it was the end of the day, to which responded, why am I

 under arrest then. the white officer just laughed. a moment or two later I noticed that the

 white office had a CD with the paperwork.. I asked if that was the video of the incident.

 the white officer said yes, he said it was hard to tell in the video, but it looks like the SUV

 did hit me and then he laughed. I then got loud and asked why I was being arrested, what

 are the charges and the white officer said what I did was a felony and asked me again if I

 was on drugs and then told me, the white officers exact




words were If you have an issue, you need to go to 6 Fuller st in Chicopee and settle it

there). it was at this point that I decided to stop talking... I was very pissed off that I wasn't

 read my rights and that they both refused to tell me what I was being charged with and I

 didn't want to say anything to get myself into more trouble, even though I don't have any

 idea why I was arrested or what the driver said to the police that made them ignore my

 side of the incident. it was a 15-20 minute ride to the station and I told both officers twice

 that I need medical attention both ignored me. it was only once we pulled up to the garage

 door that I asked again and the white officer said they would call an ambulance to check

 me out and see if I need treatment. the two officers then brought me into booking and left..

 I then informed both of the officers on duty that I need  medical attention. they both said

 someone will be in to see me shortly. when I realized no one made any calls for medical

 attention, I stopped answering the officers questions regarding my name and any other

 information. they then both told me that I wouldn't be released on bail if I didn't answer. I

 then asked what i was being charged with and they didn't respond. shortly after

removing my personal items, I was asked take pictures of the cut on my hand and to follow

 a officer to have my finger prints scanned, it turns out that I had seen this officer before at

 a children's learn to skate program that I took my nephew to at forest park just a month

 before. I told him about my injuries and he was very careful not to cause further damage

 to my wrists and shoulder.. I told him that the cuffs caused me severe pain and what felt

 like more tearing in both my wrists and that I had previous injuries to both and only had

 surgery in my left wrist and that up until I got cuffed, I had been totally pain free, I then

 told this officer I need to see a Dr and was still in a terrible amount of pain. he told me that

 the officers at the desk will take care of me and I then went to my cell. I sat in that cell

 until 9pm and did not get any medical attention. at 9pm I was released on bail. the officers

 were all pissed off at me and were threatening not to





release me because I wrote I need medical attention on the paperwork dealing with my

personal property. the court clerk was there and he told me that I was only being released

 because the police report was one sided.. the audio and video from the jail will prove

 everything I am stating. I then spent the next 5 and a half hours in the emergency room

 just so I could get something for my pain and have X-rays taken, when I could've went to

 my primary Dr right after the incident, I believe I was falsely arrested. I wrote this while

 in the ER for 5 hours waiting to see a Dr, so I apologize for any spelling or grammar

 errors. January 17+0

HAMPDEN, ss.
SIGNED AND SEALED IN MY PRESENCE.

Richard L Stebbins Jr.
Laura Stebbins
Linda Stebbins
Mark a. Festa


Notary Public
Commonwealth of Massachusetts
My Commission Expires:
July 29, 2016

 Talked out of a lawyer, I told ADA Katharine Johnston that I was sick, I was hurting and I gave her copies of my medical files, so she knew i had torn rotator cuff and was denied medical attention. and she told me I was going to have to wait around all day long for a court appointed lawyer or I could agree to represent that myself and I could go home right now

for some reason – hasn’t viewed the evidence, the

didn’t see it.

Mart.

MR. STEBBINS: I brought it three – three days

later, on a Monday, ‘cause I only got it on Friday.

So, they have the evidence and – for some reason –

THE COURT OFFICER: If we could address Richard Stebbins.
THE COURT: Mr. Stebbins, have you made up your mind as to
how you want to proceed?
MR. STEBBINS: I waive the jury, Your Honor.
THE COURT: All right. Commonwealth, your witnesses are
available, are here, ready to go?
THE COMMONWEALTH: Yes, Your Honor. We have one police
officer and one civilian witness, who’s the victim.
THE COURT: All right. Before we go on tape, let me ask
Mr. Stebbins, I need to go over this with you. I looked earlier:
I showed you that form – that jury waiver form that you signed.
And that indicates, first of all, that you’re going to waive your
right to an attorney.
THE CLERK: Your Honor, if I could swear him in, I didn’t
do that.
THE COURT: Okay.
THE CLERK: Please raise your right-hand, sir.
MR. STEBBINS: I do.
THE CLERK: Thank you, sir.
THE COURT: Now, you signed a waiver of counsel form, first
of all. And you understood that you have the right to be
represented by an attorney and if you could not afford one, the
4
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
Court could appoint one or you could hire one and you chose to
represent yourself, correct?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, sir.
THE COURT: And I just want to make sure you understand that.
As you probably know, a lawyer is someone with some special
training and experience, who can usually give advice and guidance
to someone facing criminal charge like this. You have an
absolute, constitutional right to represent yourself, but when
you do that, you run certain risks. The outcome may not be
exactly what you expected. You may overlook some defense. You
may overlook some future consequence of what happens here today.
First of all – so, how old are you?
MR. STEBBINS: Thirty—four.
THE COURT: How far did you go in school?
MR. STEBBINS: College, second year of college.
THE COURT: And today, are you under the influence of alcohol,
drugs or medicine?
MR. STEBBINS: No.
THE COURT: Do you suffer from any sort of illness that might be
impairing your judgment and thinking?
MR. STEBBINS: No.
THE COURT: So, with regard to an attorney, you understand
that that’s the way you want to proceed, you won’t be able to
change your mind later on. Do you still want to waive your right
to an attorney and represent yourself?
5
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
MR. STEBBINS: Yes.
THE COURT: All right. Now, with regard to the waiver of
the jury trial -- do we have a waiver for a jury trial form?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes. It’s right here.
THE COURT: Can you take a look at that, because I need to have
that in writing as well.
MS. JOHNSTON: Yes, Judge.
THE COURT: All right. So this form indicates that you’ve
chosen to be tried by a Judge, without a jury. Did you read this
before you sign it?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, sir.
THE COURT: Did you understand what it says?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, sir.
THE COURT: All right. You have a right to have one trial
on these two charges, which have been brought against you. That
trial could – again – be in front of a Judge or a jury. If you
wanted to have a jury trial, we’d bring in people from the
community, they wouldn’t really know about you or about this
matter ahead of time. You could work to pick six of those
people. You could challenge any two of those people, without
giving any reason, to try to assure that they are fair and
impartial.
If you chose to have a trial in front of a jury, the jurors
would listen to the witnesses, look at any evidence, listen to
the Judge instruct them on the law. Then they would return a
6
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
unanimous verdict of guilty or not guilty on each of these
charges. So, all six jurors would have to agree that you are
guilty, before you could be found guilty.
When you waive or you give up your right to a jury trial and you
allow a Judge to decide, both the facts and the law and whether
you are guilty. It really is a practical matter that one persons
decision as to the facts would be final. So, having said that,
do you understand the difference between a trial in front of a
Judge and a trial in front of a jury?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, Your Honor.
THE COURT: All right. Do you have any questions about
that issue?
MR. STEBBINS: No, Your Honor.
THE COURT: Is it still your wish – after I’ve explained
that to you – to waive your right to a jury trial on the charges
of malicious damage to a motor vehicle and disorderly conduct and
instead, have that trial in front of a single Judge?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, Your Honor.
THE COURT: And you understand that, once you’ve made that
decision, you won’t be able to change your mid later on?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, Your Honor.
THE COURT: All right. So you made a voluntary, knowing,
intelligent waiver of your right to counsel and your right to a
jury trial and I’ll allow that.
The way this is going to proceed is, I’ll just – I can’t give
7
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
you, obviously, any legal advice, but I would just say of ways to
proceed, the Commonwealth obviously has the burden of proof,
proving guilt beyond a reasonable doubt. The Commonwealth can,
if the prosecutor wishes to, make a statement of what she expects
the evidence is going to be. If you want to, you can make an
opening statement. You don’t have to, because the burden of
proof is on the Commonwealth. You can make an opening statement
at the beginning of the trial or you can make it later in the
trial or you can make no opening statement. That’s, again, up to
you.
After the opening statement or statements – if there are any
– then the Commonwealth’s going to produce – present evidence.
He will listen to the evidence. If you wish to object, because
you feel something is not admissible under our rules of evidence,
you would just stand up and say the word “objection”. I’ll hear
from you. Then I’m going to give you the right to cross—examine
any witness who testifies against you.
Then the Commonwealth, when it’s done presenting the evidence,
the prosecutor will rest and it’ll be up to you to decide whether
you wish to present any evidence. You’re not obligated to do
that.
Again, the law doesn’t require a person to prove his or her
innocence. The burden is on the Commonwealth to prove that
someone is guilty and prove it beyond a reasonable doubt.
If you wish to present evidence and you’ll testify or produce
8
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
whatever evidence you wish and the prosecutor will have a right
to cross—examine you. All I ask is that both parties just sort
of stay on point, be respectful, civil. The rules of evidence,
the rules of procedure, apply to both sides evenly, even though
you’re representing yourself, you’re still bound by the same
rules – your conduct of evidence in the law and the Commonwealth.
Do you have any questions about any of that?
MR. STEBBINS: No, Your Honor.

continued down the side of her car, slamming his fists and the
milk jug, which then flew. At this time, Your Honor, she said
she put the car in park. That she turned around to now take
attention to this defendant who, at the time by her testimony,
was making racial slurs at her. That he had noticed her race and
ethnicity at this time and that he was making statements to her
to – that were hostile towards her.
At this time, Your Honor, she watched this defendant take
his arm and slam it through the rear of her vehicle, causing the
damages that you have seen to her rear window. That the glass
was shattered around the parking lot and the photos of the
47
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
defendant’s injuries as a result.
You heard from this victim. She testified that she was scared.
She was upset by this. That the defendant was very angry,
aggressive and assaultive towards her. And that after getting
out of her car, she parked, there were other people around. And
she remained away from this defendant, because she was fearful
after this incident had taken place.
You also heard, Your Honor, from Officer Sheehan. Upon his
arrival at the scene, he testified that he wasn’t sure what was
going on with the defendant. He thought he might be either,
under the influence or have some sort of mental issue, as he was
acting odd. And that his characterization of the defendant at
the scene was that he was belligerent.
His view of the evidence is consistent with the testimony of the
witness in this case, the damages that you saw, the injuries that
he sustained. The officer testified that, upon arrival, this
victim was hysterical, that she was crying and upset. And that
was as a result of this incident that had just taken place with
Mr. Stebbins.
As a result, Your Honor, the defendant was charged malicious
damage to a motor vehicle. As Your Honor knows, that requires
the Commonwealth to prove that this defendant did injure or
destroy a motor vehicle, belonging to another person. And more
importantly, that he did so with malice. I don’t think there’s
any question that it was a motor vehicle, that there was damage
48
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
done, leaving us with the remaining question of whether it was
done with malice.
To show that, Your Honor, the Commonwealth needs to prove
that there was some sort cruelty, hostility or revenge. It does
not need to prove that this defendant knew who this victim was.
So, all that’s left is, was there cruelty, was there hostility,
was there some revenge act going on here? I think there’s
credibility testimony from the witness and corroborating
testimony from Officer Sheehan that this defendant was hostile
and acting in a cruel manner towards this witness through – this
victim, excuse me – throughout their interaction. And that the
Commonwealth has sustained it’s burden in that respect.
As to the disorderly conduct, Your Honor, again, that would
require us to prove that this defendant engaged in some sort of
threatening behavior or some sort of violent or tumultuous
behavior or created some sort of physical – psychically offensive
situation that didn’t serve any legitimate purpose. I think the
Commonwealth could sustain its burden on any three of those
theories of disorderly conduct.
His threatening behavior, his racial remarks that he was
making towards her, you heard the witness say that, after this
happened, he said he would crack her skull. He called her
numerous things throughout that Your Honor heard. In terms of
his violent, tumultuous behavior, I think that same evidence
supports that, in addition to the actual malicious act of
49
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Providence, RI
breaking out her back window.
And finally, I can’t think of any legitimate purpose for his
actions. Your Honor, even if you were to believe the defendant
that he had been struck by the car or something to that effect,
that does not give him a legitimate purpose to then break in her
windshield. In fact, that would further support that this was
done in revenge if Your Honor did view the evidence in that way.
However, Your Honor, I believe the Commonwealth has met its
burden. We would ask you to find the defendant guilty of both,
the charge of malicious damage of a motor vehicle and the
disorderly conduct, based on the testimony of the witnesses.

THE CLERK: Your Honor, how much time for the $90
victim/witness?
THE COURT: We’ll give Mr. Stebbins 30 days to pay that.
Actually, I’ll make it 60 days to pay that.
56

THE COURT: To July 1st. All right.
PROBATION: Judge, did you want the mental health or --
THE COURT: No – anger management. I checked anger
management.

you.
THE COURT: All right, thank you.

MOTION TO SET ASIDE THE VERDICT AND

AND ORDER A NEW TRIAL

Now comes the Defendant in the above-entitled case, and pursuant to Mass. R. Crim.

P. 25(b)(2), respectfully requests this Honorable Court to set aside the verdicts and to

direct findings of not guilty, on in the alternative, pursuant to Mass. R. Crim. P. 30, to

vacate the conviction and order a new trial.

As grounds therefore, the Defendant states that the verdict are so far against the

current of evidence that to allow them to stand would perpetrate a serious injustice. The

Defendant, who appeared pro se on the day of trial, provided to the Trial Judge a video

tape of the events that took place. This evidence in connection with the facts of the case

warrant a finding of Not Guilty. In the alternative the Defendant respectfully requests that

this Honorable Court vacate the conviction and order a new trial.

FOR THE DEFENDANT,

Date: May 20,2013

AFFIDAVIT IN SUPPORT OF MOTION

I, RANDY J. MILOU, do hereby depose and say that the following is true to the best

of my knowledge, understanding and belief:

1. The Defendant appeared pro se for the Trial that took place on May 1,2013.

2. After trial the Defendant was found Guilty of (1) Malicious Damage to a

Motor Vehicle, and (2) Disorderly Conduct.

3. The evidence that was admitted at trial does not deem sufficient to support a

find of Guilty for either charge.

4. The Defendant provided the Judge with a video recording of the incident as it

was captured from the Walmart Security Camera. This video is consistent with

the Defendant's version of events.

5. The Defendant has also provided a statement of his version of events. This is

similar (in not the same) as the testimony provided by the Defendant at Trial.

Attached as Exhibit "A"

.

6. The Defendant is also providing a witness statement that has just been

procured.

7. This affidavit was prepared solely for purposes of litigating this motion and

does not contain all of the information known to the Defendant. Attached as

Exhibit "B".

EXHIBIT "A"

On Wednesday January 16th at 14:03:58 I began my exit from Walmart in Springfield, MA the

weather conditions were an icy rain with a slight wind .. I walked toward the row of cars where I

was parked and started on from what my perspective was the right side of the row. In the

meantime a vehicle began backing out of a space as I was walking towards it. I don't believe they

saw me, but they were backing out slow enough that I felt safe in crossing over to the left side of

the row, my car was parked on the left side, farther away from the front of the store, so crossing

seemed the logical thing to do. I made it to the left side of the row before the vehicle backing up

finished reversing, At which point I looked up to see what was in front of me. I saw a group of

approximately six people on the same side I was on, a woman and some younger children and I

saw a white SUV slowed almost to a stop in the middle 113ofthe isle approximately 30 feet

away from me with the driver on what appeared to be a cell phone. So I continued my walk

towards my car and the group of children looking down because of the icy conditions and seeing

that there was presently trash thrown on the ground by customers ofWalmart and more

importantly I didn't want to slip and fall on ice covered pavement. A few moments later I was

struck by the right mirror and the body of the same SUV that had been a safe distance away

shortly before, I never saw it coming, the driver must have swerved towards me because I didn't

weave into the middle of the road while walking. the mirror hit my right bicep and the body of

the SUV hit the gallon of milk I had in my hand causing it to hit my right leg and twist it to the

point that the milk jugs handle split and cut my right hand, I felt a huge surge of pain and tearing

in my right shoulder joint and right bicep and I felt tearing in my right wrist, the pain was instant

and I began seeing stars, the impact though it wasn't in my opinion very fast still caused me to

lose my balance and the white SUV's rear right tire ran over the side of my right foot and it wasat this moment that the driver chose to stop trapping my right shoe under the edge of the tire (I

didn't know it at the time, but her tire only ran over my shoe and not my flesh and bone foot). So

in a panic yelled move your car you're on my foot and I swung my right elbow at the rear

passenger side of the SUV hoping to get the drivers attention, the milk jug I was holdings

remaining contents went everywhere and the jug went flying and I was yanking as hard as I

could to get my leg free and then I yelled move your car again and swung my elbow at the side

of the vehicle again and while I was swinging I was able to yank my foot free, but I stumbled

and my elbow hit the bottom right comer of the rear windshield instead causing it to shatter

(which shouldn't have happened, it was either the luckiest shot in the world or there was already

a defect in the window) , at that point I could see through the now broken window and there was

a cell phone in the drivers right hand and I yelled- get off your cell phone, you just hit me with

your mirror and stopped your car on my foot ,watch where you're driving. the driver said

something either to me or to the person on the phone, but I don't recall what they said because I

was so freaked out. the driver then began to drive away, so I turned to walk to my car (I was in

so much pain that I wasn't in any condition to chase the vehicle down and a moment later I heard

yelling behind me, I then turned around to see that the driver had decided to stop and I then

yelled call the police and we'll deal with this when they get here and I started walking towards

my car because I didn't have my cell phone on me to call police and I knew there was no point in

arguing with this person about it and seeing that her window was broken in my attempt to free

my foot from under her tire, I didn't want to approach her and make her feel that I was in anyway

placing her in danger or that I was trying to intimidate her. The driver yelled some more and I

yelled back call the police and then we'll talk. I then placed my remaining groceries into my car

and grabbed a fishing vest I had in my trunk to help stop the bleeding on my right hand. The painin my wrist and shoulder was building every moment and I could feel swelling in my right bicep

where the mirror hit me as I stood around waiting for officers to show up. while waiting I was

approached by a few customers and Walmart security all asking if I was okay. I explained to

them that I had been hit by the white SUV that was now parked in the isle towards the store and

that I was in severe pain and I felt dizzy. I explained to the security guard that I had a previous

injury to my right wrist and had surgery on my right shoulder, but up until the moment I was hit,

I had been totally pain free in these areas because of therapy with my wrist and a successful

surgery to correct the issue with my right shoulder. I asked the security guard to tell the police

this too. I didn't want anyone thinking I was trying to fake an injury or that I was trying to claim

to have been in 100% perfect condition before this incident happened. After what felt like

forever the police arrived and arrested me.

Prepared by, Richard Stebbins





EXHIBIT B




It was a cold winter day and it was snowy on January 16, 2013. I Sheldon Thompson witness day accident at the Walmart parking lot. I observed Mr. Stebbins walking through the parking lot heading to the main road. I was on the other side of the vehicles. I then noticed a white SUV driving through the parking lot. The SUV was driving fast thru lanes. As I walked by by the SUV it tried to pass around and then drove forward at this time, I heard Mr. Stebbins yell at the driver of the vehicle. I kept on walking to my vehicle that I heard a loud bang and heard Mr. Stebbins screaming out loud. He stopped your car on my foot. As I looked at Mr. Stebbins he had fell forward and was trying to get up from the ground. The driver of the SUV that exited out of her vehicle and started yelling. I don’t give a fuck you’re going to pay for my motherfucking window. Mr. Stebbins yelled back get off your cell. You just hit me. And then walked to Mr. Stebbins to see if he needed to go to the emergency room. Mr. Stebbins stated once to Police came and got his information. I then gave Mr. Stebbins my cell number if you needed a witness. Then I left the scene. Sheldon Thompson.

June 5, 2013

SO# 13-019

Dear Mr. Stebbins, In regard to the complaint you filed on March 7, 2013, please be advised that the subsequent investigation and reports were reviewed.

In order for you to understand our internal investigation process I should give you some background. Through various sources the Springfield Police Department receives a Citizen's Complaint. Based upon the complaint I will order an investigation. Generally that investigation is conducted by the Internal Investigations Unit. When the case is thoroughly investigated it is then brought before the Community Police Hearing Board. The Board consists of seven members of the community, residents of Springfield and appointed by the Mayor, with the authority to hear internally investigated cases and offers recommendations to me as to their findings. It is then investigation the commanding officer makes a recommendation regarding the case. The case is then reviewed by a deputy police chief who then makes a recommendation again based upon the facts brought forth in the case. The case is then brought for my review. I make a determination based upon the facts presented. I can accept, reject or modify the recommendations of the commanding officer and the deputy chief. By contractual agreements with sworn personnel I have a certain amount of time within which I can act regarding disciplinary matters. 

In this particular case, on January 16, 2013 you were arrested. According to your complaint, you were walking in the parking lot of Wal-Mart when you were struck by a vehicle. This caused a series of events which also resulted in damage the rear window of the vehicle. You stated that you suffered injury to preexisting conditions. You complained that you told the officers on scene and also in the booking area about your injuries and the need for medical attention but received none.

At the time you submitted your complaint you also signed a HIPPA medical release form to allow the investigator access to your medical records.

About a week later you were interviewed by the assigned investigator. You provided an incident report from Wal-Mart security and a website address that contained a video recording of the incident. You performed a reenactment of the incident in an effort to clarify some points to the investigator. 

You offered another complaint about the officers. You stated that they did not identify themselves as police officers when they arrived on scene. The investigator mentioned that the fact that they were in uniform and arrived in a marked police cruiser seemed to suffice as identification. 

You offered that it is possible for someone to have a fake uniform and equipment. You told the investigator that after you were bailed out of jail you were driven to your mother's house and from there went to the hospital with your wife. You also advised the investigator of your intent to sue the individuals involved in the matter for filing a false police report. 

The medical documents were reviewed. The documents indicated that the X-rays provided no indication of new injury. There was note of indentations on your wrists. The documents also noted that you informed the physician that you purposely struck the rear window of the vehicle with your elbow because the driver did not stop after striking you. When the investigator inquired as to this information you did not have a reason why you told the doctor that. 

The investigator interviewed witnesses regarding the matter. One witness' attention was drawn because you were yelling at the person in the SUV. The witness heard you swearing, making racist comments and saw you striking the vehicle on the passenger side repeatedly with a milk jug. You were described as two to three feet away from the vehicle and only stopped striking the vehicle with the milk jug when it burst open. The witness stated that your foot was never struck by the vehicle. The witness also described you as walking over to the vehicle and punching the rear window causing it to shatter. 

The investigator interviewed another witness who corroborated the account of the previously mentioned witness. 

The operator of the vehicle offered that it is her belief that you struck her vehicle as she drove slowly by. She commented that you then kept striking her vehicle on the passenger side with the milk jug. You then punched the vehicle and finally shattered the rear window. She also recalled that you called her a racist name and threatened to "crack" her skull. 

The security for Wal-Mart was interviewed and stated that he thought that the incident was a domestic dispute. He stated that the next day you asked him for an incident report and he quickly authored one for you. 

The booking video recording was reviewed. Throughout the recording there was no indication that you were experiencing any discomfort in the areas of the body that you allege were injured in the incident. You are observed washing the cut to your hand and being offered first aid by the booking sergeant. There is audio recording of the sergeant telling you that someone would look at your injured hand and determine if you need further medical treatment at a hospital. 

The booking release video recording was reviewed. You did not appear to have any discomfort when bending and putting your clothes back on. You are also observed talking and moving your hands in an apparently pain free manner.


The booking photographs show the hand abrasion. It also shows your wrists which do not appear to have the indentations that were described in your previously mentioned medical records. 

The individual who fingerprinted you was interviewed. He stated that, while he does not remember you specifically, when a person asks for medical treatment it is his normal practice to inform the booking sergeant or one of the booking officers. 

The booking personnel stated that you showed no evidence of injury other than your hand abrasion and you did not ask for medical treatment beyond the first aid that was administered at the time of booking. The officers on the next shift noted that you wrote on the property slip as you were being released that you wanted medical treatment. When one of the officers asked why you wrote that, you informed him that you wanted medical treatment. The officer advised you to seek the treatment upon release which was minutes away. 

The complained of officers were interviewed and denied any unprofessional actions. They recount being dispatched to the scene and observing the damage to the vehicle. They saw you standing back with a cloth wrapped around your right hand. The officers interviewed the individuals and made a determination to arrest you. The officers also viewed and obtained a copy of the Wal-Mart parking lot surveillance video. The video recording showed you striking the vehicle with the milk jug and then punching the rear window causing it to shatter. The officers stated that, once under arrest and being transported to the police station for booking, you told them that if you were being arrested you wanted to go to the hospital to have your hand stitched. You then laughed. 

The officers stated that they would tell the booking sergeant and the decision to transport you to a hospital would be made there. The officers noted that they told the sergeant of your hand abrasion. They deny your allegation that the handcuffs were too tight on your wrists. The officers stated that they took the precaution of double locking the handcuffs to avoid them closing further on you wrists. 

After careful consideration of the results of this investigation, it was determined that the case was not sustained. The Springfield Police Department will take no further action. 

Please be assured that the Springfield Police Department takes seriously allegations made against its members. We therefore appreciate your taking the time to bring your concerns to our

attention.

Sincerely,

William J. Fitchet

William J. Fitchet

Police Commissioner

THE COURT: Mr. Stebbins, you can come on up, please.
Looks like you filed two motions - a motion for a new trial and
another motion for some sort of discover, so, go ahead, I’ll hear
from you. By the way, are you represented by a lawyer in this
matter?
MR. STEBBINS: No.
THE COURT: All right.
MR. STEBBINS: My appeals lawyer said she doesn’t deal with
evidence that’s been tampered with, only appeals, so I’d be
representing myself.
THE COURT: Okay, go ahead, I’ll hear from you.
MR. STEBBINS: During my trial, I was denied evidence that
day. I was told that you had denied my request for her motor
vehicle accident report of the day, Ms. Johnson’s (phonetic) car
insurance information, the audio and video from the police
station and her phone records.
THE COURT: Hold on a second - you were told that I denied
your request?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, Your Honor, by the Assistant District
Attorney. I’ve already been - I was already approved it by Judge
Beattie, I believe it was. I don’t have the audio recordings - I
have the request here from December 5th - I’ve been still waiting
4
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
on these recordings - showing that I was given access to this
information. It was never provided for me. And, on, uh, May
10th, I’d also - I requested her insurance information and I
don’t recall your exact answer, but I was denied that request.
Since then, I’ve been proven - I’ve been shown evidence my police
report was tampered with. Ms. Johnson (phonetic) did not have
car insurance at the time of the accident and her registration
was suspended.
When I talked to the Registry, her insurance and
registration was cancelled on December 17th - a full month before
the incident. She was driving illegally or she shouldn’t have
been driving at all, that car shouldn’t have been there, when she
claimed I assaulted her. She reinstated her registration six
days later, on the 22nd. And she was still driving that vehicle
to and from work, knowing that the registration had been
suspended.
She had worked for my mother-in-law at the time of this
incident. That’s how I know she was still driving the vicinity.
I have the police report here and to show what evidence was
deleted off the police report, and there’s officers and troopers
in the room here that can attest to the screen that they see when
they pull up an individual’s registration information that is
missing from the report, but it has her VIN number, which they
wouldn’t have been able to just remember that. Judge, I would’ve
had no way to defend properly, without this information that I
5
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
requested. And at the time, I thought this was just random
information, not that it would be so important to my case.
THE COURT: How is this so important to your case?
MR. STEBBINS: She accused me for absolutely no reason of
assaulting her vehicle, while I was walking through a parking
lot, which I claim that she hit me.
THE COURT: Okay.
MR. STEBBINS: She knew at the time she did not have car
insurance and in her panic, accused me of attacking her vehicle.
She was allowed to drive away from the scene. I’ve already
talked to the Registry. The officers involved did access her
registration information, they knew her registration was
suspended and she had no car insurance. She wasn’t cited and she
was still allowed to drive that vehicle away from the scene
illegally.
THE COURT: And you’re alleging that, in the discovery you
got in the case, which consisted of a police report --
MR. STEBBINS: Yes.
THE COURT: -- that there was some information --
MR. STEBBINS: The vehicle information was tampered with.
THE COURT: -- withheld from you?
MR. STEBBINS: Yes, it was deleted off the screen.
THE COURT: Deleted off the screen?
MR. STEBBINS: At the bottom of - I could show you the
paper, I’ve circled it.

THE COURT: All right. Attorney Johnston?
MS. JOHNSTON: Your Honor, I’m not sure if you recall, but
this is a pro se trial that proceeded in front of Your Honor back
in May, a Bench Trial. What I can tell you Your Honor is that
there was an arraignment date in January. At that time, the
9
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
defendant requested from us that we get the Wal-Mart video. We
did do that; that was provided to him. He was provided all the
discovery that existed.
When there is a motor vehicle accident report, it’s
typically noted in the police report, in the narrative, with the
ending, “Dash-AC”, which is an accident report. Those are
typically then requested from the police department. This
defendant was provided all discovery that was available to the
Commonwealth, as far as I’m aware, there were no pretrial
conference reports, indicating that there was anything missing
that we would be provided to him.
He decided to go pro se to trial. Back in March, I think,
there was a discussion of him having a Bar Advocate appointed.
However, he then waived that and asked for a quick trial date in
May. We proceeded to trial. There were no motions to dismiss or
discovery motions or anything brought before Your Honor on that
day. We went to trial. Your Honor found him guilty. There was
then a motion for reconsideration of that filed and a motion for
appeal. He’s continued to file motions, both with our Appellate
Unit with our office and, I believe, the Springfield Police
Department, seeking what I would consider at this point, post
conviction discovery.
At this point, Your Honor, I would - I guess treat this as a
Rule 30 motion for a new trial. However, I’m not sure we’ve
received a motion for a new trial. The appropriate way to go
10
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
about his would be to file a motion for a new trial. In that
motion for a new trial, there would be need to be some level of
belief by Your Honor that there would be a likelihood of success,
if the information that he sought was obtained or even existed.
And under that section of the - I believe it’s C4, Rule 30, C4,
post conviction discovery - it basically has to say that that
discovery would somehow rise to a level of him having some
success, if he were able to obtain that post conviction
discovery.
In the case, I can’t see how, whether or not the car that he
is - has been found guilty of punching in the rear window in the
parking lot - whether it makes a difference whether that car was
insured or not insured or registered or not registered or whether
the driver had a license or not. Those are not issues that were
in the case.
The case was, did he maliciously damage that vehicle. We
went to trial, Your Honor heard the evidence, you saw the video
yourself. And I can say that, in the wake of this, he then filed
some claims with various insurance companies, which I believe
were denied. And I just - from the Commonwealth’s prospective,
if this is to be looked at as a motion for new trial and a motion
for post conviction discovery, I don’t see how these documents
that have been proved to you - and before the Commonwealth -
would even begin to meet the standard to, first of all, allow
Your Honor to order that post conviction discovery, if it even
11
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
exits, which I don’t believe it does. And then to allow a - what
I would say an evidentiary - some sort of an evidentiary hearing,
prior to Your Honor considering the granting of a motion for new
trial.
What I can say is, you’ve already heard the trial, you heard
the motion for a reconsideration of that trial and the appeal, I
believe, has been filed. So, I would ask Your Honor to deny this
request. As I said, I don’t think the documents that have been
filed procedurally meet the guidelines. This is a defendant who
has decided to go pro se, both at trial and post trial. He’s to
be held to the same standard as any other attorney would be, by
doing so. And as such, I think, first procedurally, it’s not
proper. And, second, if he were to even meet the merits of it,
that it doesn’t raise to the standard set forth by the rule, and
therefore, I’d ask you to deny it.
THE COURT: Let me ask you, Attorney Johnston, as far as

MS. JOHNSTON: Your Honor, this is a typically police
report, it was completed on the day of the incident. It’s an
arrest report, therefore, it’s completed that day, either at the
end of shift or after the incident, unlike a warrant report,
which could be technically completed later, but because there was
an arrest, it’s completed that day.
THE COURT: Mm-hmm.
MS. JOHNSTON: It’s as - from the Commonwealth’s
13
Catuogno Court Reporting & StenTel Transcription
Springfield, MA Worcester, MA Boston, MA Chelmsford, MA Providence, RI
prospective, a full and accurate reporting of what took place
that day. The officer types in all the information. He gives
the information of the parties, of the vehicles. As I stated, it
doesn’t reference any accident report in there. It does
reference various other things, such as her registration or an
evidence tag. There’s no reason to believe that anything
would’ve been deleted or that there would’ve been any sort of
intent to withhold information from this defendant. That’s not a
commonplace thing that we come across.
What I can tell Your Honor is that, these police reports,
they are a full and accurate representation of what the officer
views, at the time of the incident, to be the important facts,
regarding the charges that are going to enter. I am not aware,
Your Honor - I have not done the leg work that Mr. Stebbins has
done, to go to the RMV, to pull up her records, to find her
insurance, to do all of those things - that is not a typically
discovery request that I would be making.
If Mr. Stebbins had requested that from us --
THE COURT: I’m not suggesting you wouldn’t.
MS. JOHNSTON: To be honest, I’m not even sure that we
would. I think that would be Rule 17 motion that he would have
to file to get those kind of records. They’re not in the care
and custody and control of the Commonwealth. It’s not something
that we would’ve had access to ourselves. So, quite frankly, I’m
not sure how he even got some of that information.

From my prospective - from the Commonwealth’s prospective -
this is an accurate police report with full information about the
incident that took place that day. Her information is there. I
would be surprised to find out that there would’ve been something
on here that some officer would’ve gone in and specifically
deleted in some way, to withhold information from Mr. Stebbins.
I think there’s no basis for that allegation. It's
commonplace that vehicle information goes in here, with a VIN
number, with an owner, with the status of it. It’s not going to
give all of their insurance information. It’s not going to give
all of the various details of their insurance, their
registration. It’s not a traffic ticket. She wasn’t given a
traffic ticket.
I don’t see, as I said, any pretrial conference reports, any
discovery requests, any notations in my file, that these types of
things were requested from Mr. Stebbins. I’ve looked at the

I don’t remember any mention of it on the day or trial. As Your
Honor typically sees things like we just saw with the drug serve
(phonetic), we’re here on the day of trial and defense counsel is
asking for a motion to dismiss, because discovery wasn’t
provided. That did not take place on the day of trial for Mr.
Stebbins trial.
So, from my prospective, this is a full and complete police

It's something that I think would be the basis of a Rule 17

He told my better leave right now and then he prays I get some help. He said another woman came and asked for a copy of the receipt and said she needed it for court and she never came back and paid for the window either. I'm lucky that guy didn't knock me out he was way bigger than I was and he looked like he was pissed and I was rude and disrespectful and I don't know, I have no excuse for my behavior I was sick at the time so it was my fault i jumped to conclusions without asking him. thankfully I got him to write that statement before I pissed him off. lol

He went on and on how good my evidence was, I had the police report that was missing her car insurance information, I had verification that her car insurance had been suspended, the police complaint that was missing my actual medical information, I had the paperwork from Brandon at the registry that shows Kishana had been committing insurance and tax fraud, then just ignoring the fact that everyone else has to pay and I thought someone was helping her.


They didn't just remember her address, when they told me on the car ride back to the station, that report proves that those officers ran her license plate, they knew her vehicle didn't have insurance, had a rejection sticker for safety and her registration was suspended. plus I told Nolan about the time I saw Kishana pulled over on Slater Ave. and how that officer let her drive away as well.
